### PR #168 — Decide connector secret lifecycle data model (Model B) · 2026-06-24
- **Category:** design/spec — **docs-only.** No migration, no code, no test, no runtime event constant, no dependency. Tests unchanged 548; RLS unchanged 550; types unchanged 1837; no doc 17 §5 box ticked. **Resolves the rotation/revocation/tombstone data model and gives the next implementation PR exact instructions; implements NO lifecycle behavior.**
- **DECISION — Model B (doc 42 §85):** lifecycle state lives in a **separate, INSERT-only `connector_secret_lifecycle_events` table**, NOT in a mutable `connector_secrets.status`. **`connector_secrets` stays append-only — no UPDATE, no DELETE, no new UPDATE grant** (the RISK-007 invariant T50 protects). Rotation = a new higher-`version` `connector_secrets` row (immutable old row); revocation/tombstone = an insert-only lifecycle event; revoke-without-replacement is supported.
- **Lifecycle monotonicity:** events are monotonic toward non-loadable — any `revoked` or `tombstoned` event permanently makes that `(tenant, connector, secret_kind, version)` non-loadable; there is NO unrevoke/reactivate/restore event; a terminal non-loadable event always wins.
- **Load query — fail-closed latest-INTENT (the load-bearing rule):** exact-version lookup returns the secret only if that exact version is eligible (active, non-expired, well-formed, no revoked/tombstoned event), else null. Latest-version lookup (if added) computes the **highest version across ALL rows first** (including revoked/tombstoned/superseded/expired/malformed), then evaluates **that single highest version's** eligibility — **NO fallback to a lower version; NOT "highest eligible version."** Expiry is treated as strictly as revocation (highest expired + lower valid → null). Rationale: fallback can resurrect an old credential after an intended revoke/failed rotation/expiry. All edge cases enumerated in §85.6.
- **Grants/RLS spec (for the implementation PR, not done here):** `connector_secrets` keeps NO UPDATE/DELETE grant; the lifecycle table gets narrow column-scoped grants — `connector_runner` INSERT (write events) + SELECT (eligibility checks in the load query), **never UPDATE/DELETE**; an append-only trigger rejects UPDATE/DELETE proven under `set role connector_runner`; RLS proves tenant isolation; no service-role path, no public route.
- **Atomicity spec (for the implementation PR):** each lifecycle mutation + its audit event commit atomically in ONE runner transaction (the §84 pattern); audit failure rolls back the lifecycle change and vice-versa; NO compensating DELETE; real DB tests must prove all rollback cases under the runner.
- **Audit event names RESERVED in prose only** (`connector_secret.{rotation,revocation,tombstone}.{attempted,succeeded,failed}`) — **NOT added as runtime constants** (the #166 builder still supports only the nine store/load/decrypt events; #167 wired only the six store/load events; decrypt + lifecycle events remain unwired).
- **Posture:** **No lifecycle behavior is implemented yet. Real connector credential storage/use is still NOT allowed; rotation/revocation behavior and the real credential lifecycle remain missing. Connector credentials are not production-ready. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§85), 41 (§94), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` 548/548; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff (1837); linked ref confirmed `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai` (local read); docs-only diff.

### PR #167 — Wire connector secret store/load audit emission (atomic, fail-closed) · 2026-06-24
- **Category:** RISK-007 audit wiring. Wires the #166 allowlist audit builder into the #160 runner-backed store adapter so store/load operations emit a **persisted** audit row, **fail-closed**. Adds migration **`0031`** (narrow audit-INSERT grant) + RLS **T52**; new `src/lib/server/connector-vault/secret-audit-writer.ts` (pure statement-builder) + rewritten `connector-secret-store.ts` (transactional + audited) + comprehensive tests; registers the writer in `no-client-import.test.ts`. Tests 539 → **548**; RLS suite **525 → 550** (T52); generated types unchanged 1837; migration `0031`; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **STORE is ATOMIC (the amendment's hard requirement):** `insertEncryptedSecret` runs the `connector_secrets` INSERT and its `connector_secret.store.succeeded` audit INSERT in ONE runner transaction (`begin … commit`) on ONE connection as `connector_runner`. The secret row commits ONLY if its audit row commits; if the audit INSERT fails the WHOLE transaction rolls back — **no orphaned, unaudited secret, and NO compensating DELETE.** `store.attempted` (before) and `store.failed` (on a rolled-back failure) are audit-only writes, each fail-closed. **LOAD is fail-closed by ordering:** `findEncryptedSecret` writes `load.succeeded` (or `load.failed` with a static class) before returning; if the audit write fails the load throws and the caller receives **no** secret/envelope.
- **The audit writer ENLISTS in the runner transaction** — `secret-audit-writer.ts` turns a #166 record into the `audit_logs` INSERT `{sql, params}` the store splices into its own `runSequence` (same connection/role/tx). It opens NO connection, uses NO separate role, owns NO RPC/transaction (the amendment's precondition 2).
- **Migration `0031` — smallest safe grant:** COLUMN-scoped INSERT for `connector_runner` on `public.audit_logs`, EXACTLY `(tenant_id, action, resource_type, after_json)`. audit_logs ONLY, INSERT ONLY; no select/update/delete/truncate; no `actor_user_id`/`before_json`/`resource_id`/`ip_address`/`user_agent`; no other table broadened (connector_secrets still no UPDATE/DELETE). The 0002 append-only trigger still blocks mutation for the runner. **T52 proves** the grant shape + the atomic commit (both rows) + the atomic rollback (audit failure → no connector_secrets row, no compensating delete) + append-only — all under the real `set role connector_runner`.
- **Allowlist glue:** the adapter passes the #166 builder ONLY the explicit identity it picks (`tenant_id`, `connector_id`, `secret_kind`, `version`, actor `connector_runner`, static `error_class`) — never the raw input/`encrypted` envelope. A hostile store input (secret-shaped fields) AND a benign `favoriteColor` are both structurally dropped (tested). The audit row is the #166 allowlist `after_json` only; a failed audit write surfaces a redacted static error (no secret material).
- **Scope honesty:** only the six store/load events are wired; **decrypt audit remains BUILDER-ONLY** (no decrypt call site exists); **no `rotation`/`revocation`/`delete`/`update` event** is introduced. Synthetic DB shape proven by #163; KMS/IAM decrypt separation proven by #165. **No real provider token storage. No OAuth/token exchange. No live connector execution. No request-path decrypt. No service-role secret path. No API route for secret save/load/decrypt. No UPDATE/DELETE on `connector_secrets`. No rotation/revocation.** Real credentials still blocked; rotation/revocation + real credential lifecycle remain missing. **Connector credentials are not production-ready. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§84), 41 (§93), 03, 09, 00 + `rls_test_plan.md`.

No OAuth/token exchange. No live connector execution. No request-path decrypt. No service-role secret path. No API route for secret save/load/decrypt. No UPDATE/DELETE on `connector_secrets`. ### PR #160 — Add runner-backed connector_secrets store adapter · 2026-06-24
- **Category:** wires the vault save/load boundary to the real `connector_secrets` table over the complete `0030` envelope (RISK-007 foundation). **Server-only `connector-secret-store.ts` + `connector-secret-store.test.ts` (10 tests) + RLS T51 + `no-client-import` guard hint.** No migration, no dependency; **no service-role client, no browser/request-path access, no public route, no OAuth exchange, no real provider token stored, no Okta live client, no UPDATE/DELETE on connector_secrets, no hosted command.** Tests 489 → **499**; RLS **522 → 525** (T51; T50 unchanged); types unchanged 1837 (no migration); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **What it implements** (doc 42 §78, doc 41 §89): `createRunnerConnectorSecretStore` implements the existing injected `ConnectorSecretWriteStore`/`ConnectorSecretReadStore` (secret-vault.ts) against `connector_secrets`, ONLY through the runner DB client path — every statement under **`SET ROLE connector_runner`** via the injected `RunnerConnection` (wrapped by `createRunnerDbClient`, which prepends the SET ROLE + redacts raw DB errors). Imports only `./runner-db-client` + `./secret-vault`; no Supabase client, no service-role, no `fetch`, no `process.env`, no route.
- **SAVE** inserts ONLY the 12 granted write columns — identity (`tenant_id`, `connector_id`, `secret_kind`, `version`) + the complete envelope (`ciphertext`, `dek_wrapped`, `aead_nonce`, `aad_digest`, `key_id`, `aead_tag`, `envelope_version`, `aead_alg`) via `encryptedSecretToColumns` — `RETURNING id`, fully parameterized (`$1..$12`); never `id`/`is_active`/`status`/`created_at`/`revoked_at`. The result is the row id ONLY — **no plaintext, no ciphertext**. **LOAD** selects ONLY granted columns, filters to one ACTIVE, non-expired row for (tenant, connector, kind, version) (`status = 'active' and (expires_at is null or expires_at > now())`), reconstructs the complete envelope via `columnsToEncryptedSecret`. **NO UPDATE, NO DELETE.**
- **Fail-closed:** a missing insert id, MORE THAN ONE matching active row (`limit 2`), or an INCOMPLETE/unsupported stored envelope (the mapper rejects a pre-`0030`/partial or non-v1/non-AES row) all throw a typed, secret-free error; a no-match load returns `null`. Unit tests prove SET-ROLE + parameterized SQL, only-allowed-columns (no `is_active`/`created_at`/`revoked_at`, no `id` in the insert list, no UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE), no-plaintext/ciphertext result, complete-envelope round-trip + decrypt, and the purity surface. **T51** runs the adapter's EXACT SELECT shape as `connector_runner` on real Postgres — proving every projected/filtered column (incl. `status`/`expires_at`) is grant-accessible under the `0029`/`0030` COLUMN grant AND the active/expiry/status filter is correct; **T50 is unchanged**.

No browser/request-path access is added. No public route is added. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No API key is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No real provider token is stored. No Okta live client is added. No connector sync is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is exposed to users. No browser-accessible service-role request path is added. No hosted staging/production commands were run. Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§78), 41 (§89), 09, 00.

### PR #157 — Complete connector secret encrypted envelope schema · 2026-06-23
- **Category:** completes the at-rest encrypted-envelope SHAPE for `connector_secrets` (RISK-007 foundation). **Migration `0030` (3 nullable envelope columns + extended `connector_runner` column-scoped grant) + `secret-vault.ts` column mappers + `secret-vault.test.ts` (2 new round-trip tests) + RLS T50 (extended) + verifier column-list update + regenerated types + docs.** No dependency; **no real KMS client, no live provider call, no OAuth exchange, no real token stored, no service-role path, no route/UI, no hosted command.** Tests 487 → **489**; RLS **519 → 522**; types **1828 → 1837** (the 3 new columns); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

Migration `0030` adds the 3 missing columns — **`aead_tag`** (the 16-byte GCM auth tag, REQUIRED to decrypt; `tag`), **`envelope_version`** (`v`), **`aead_alg`** (`alg`) — so every envelope field now has a column and a real encrypted secret can be persisted + loaded as a COMPLETE envelope. Mapping: `ciphertext→ciphertext, wrappedDek→dek_wrapped, iv→aead_nonce, aadDigest→aad_digest, kekId→key_id` (0017) + the 3 new.

The runner grant stays COLUMN-scoped (the #154 posture): `REVOKE ALL` then re-`GRANT SELECT/INSERT` the column sets EXTENDED with the 3 new columns — **NO table-level grant, NO UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER**; `authenticated`/`anon` keep EXACTLY zero table + column privilege; RLS-enabled, ZERO policies. T50 asserts the exact post-0030 column grants (15 SELECT / 12 INSERT) + the new aead_tag/envelope_version grants and functionally writes the complete envelope.
- **RISK-007 remains OPEN.** This completes the SCHEMA SHAPE for encrypted-envelope persistence ONLY — NOT hosted KMS/IAM separation, audit, rotation/revocation, or cutover readiness. The at-rest schema gap is now CLOSED, but remaining: the hosted KMS-grant runtime separation (mock KMS only, no real `KmsClient`); audited secret access/use; revocation/rotation/tombstone; staging verification of `0030`; production verification of `0030`; live token storage.

No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No API key is stored. No connector credentials are stored. ### PR #154 — Add connector secret vault storage/decrypt boundary · 2026-06-23
- **Category:** the highest-risk security layer — connector credential vault foundation (RISK-007). **Migration `0029` (narrow `connector_runner` `column-scoped SELECT/INSERT` grant on `connector_secrets`) + server-only `secret-vault.ts` + `secret-vault.test.ts` (15 tests) + RLS T50 + T43/T44 corrections + `no-client-import` guard hint + docs.** No dependency; **no service-role client, no real KMS client, no live provider call, no OAuth exchange, no real token stored, no route/UI, no hosted command.** Tests 472 → **487**; RLS **492 → 519**; types unchanged 1828 (grants are not columns); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **What it implements** (doc 42 §76, doc 41 §87): the connector secret VAULT storage/decrypt boundary wiring the reviewed envelope crypto (`crypto.ts`) to the `connector_secrets` store behind TWO structurally-separate capabilities. `saveConnectorSecret` (encrypt-only — `generateDataKey` only, writes ONLY ciphertext, returns a REDACTED reference with no plaintext/ciphertext); `loadConnectorSecret` (runner-only — REQUIRES a `RunnerDecryptCapability` produced only with the runner-runtime marker + a decrypt-capable provider, an unforgeable module-private-token constructor); `acquireRunnerDecryptCapability`; the `RedactedSecret` wrapper (redacts toString/toJSON/inspect; bytes only via `.expose()`). The vault imports only `./crypto` — no Supabase client, no service-role, no `fetch`, no `process.env`, no route.
- **Migration `0029`** grants `connector_runner` (NOLOGIN, BYPASSRLS, reached only via `SET ROLE`) the deferred narrow `column-scoped SELECT/INSERT` on `connector_secrets` — NO UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER. **The request-path deny-all is preserved, not weakened:** `connector_secrets` stays RLS-enabled with ZERO policies (no DELETE policy, no ALL policy); `authenticated`/`anon` keep EXACTLY zero privilege. T50 catalog-proves the exact runner grant + functional insert/select-yes, update/delete-no; T39/T40 deny-all preserved; T43/T44 corrected for the new grant. **Request-path decrypt is forbidden and tested.**
- **RISK-007 remains OPEN** (not "effectively addressed", not "mostly closed"). **Completed:** encrypted-storage primitive + save/load boundary; request-path-inaccessible secret table preserved; narrow catalog-testable runner grant; runner-only decrypt capability gate (code-level) + structural redaction; no service-role/request-path leakage. **Remaining:** the hosted KMS-grant runtime separation (runner has `kms:Decrypt`, web does not — the real cryptographic decrypt boundary) is NOT wired/verified here (mock KMS only, no real `KmsClient`); the at-rest schema gap (`connector_secrets` has no column for the GCM `tag` or `v`/`alg` format metadata yet, so a real secret cannot round-trip end to end); audited secret access/use; revocation/rotation/tombstone; staging verification; production verification; live token storage.
- **No provider API call is made. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No API key is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read in a hosted environment. No connector sync is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is exposed to users. No browser-accessible service-role request path is added. No real customer token is stored. No live Okta sync is added. No hosted staging/production commands were run. Any live verification is future work. Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§76), 41 (§87), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` **487/487**; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (18 routes); `check-auth-safety`/`check-migration-safety`/`check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `test-rls.sh` **519** (`ALL ORG-RLS ASSERTIONS PASSED`); `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff (1828 lines — grants are not columns); `node --check` on all four verifier scripts OK; linked ref confirmed `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai` (local read); no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays; no new dependency; scope: migration `0029` + RLS test (T50/T43/T44) + vault helper/tests + guard hint + docs.

### PR #153 — Add Okta discovery fact emitter · 2026-06-23
- **Category:** the FIRST real provider-data mapper — **server-only `okta-discovery-emitter.ts` + `okta-discovery-emitter.test.ts` (26 tests) + the `no-client-import` guard hint + docs.** No dependency, **no migration**; **no canonical-graph/identity-match write, no service-role client, no provider API call, no token/credential, no `connector_secrets`, no HTTP route, no hosted command, no production mutation.** Tests 446 → **472**; RLS unchanged **492**; types unchanged 1828 (no schema change); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Okta discovery fact emitter is added. Okta provider records are transformed into validated discovery facts. Okta facts are staged through the existing safe discovery fact pipeline** (doc 42 §75, doc 41 §86) — the PR #141 zod contract → the PR #142 `stageDiscoveryFactsForReview` RLS-backed staging path. High-risk (first provider data), so it maps records from an INJECTED source (real client wired later): **No live Okta sync is implemented. No provider API call is made in production.** Functions: `oktaApplicationToFacts` / `oktaUserToFacts` / `oktaAssignmentToFacts` / `emitOktaDiscoveryFacts`.
- Emits onto EXISTING schema vocabulary only: Okta app → `app_discovery` + `app_instance_identity` (external_instance_id = provider_app_id; instance_domain ONLY from explicit `settings.app.domain`); Okta user → `app_user_account` + `person_identity_candidate` (when email present); Okta assignment → `app_user_account` (user↔app, app_id_hint). `source_type` = `identity_provider_discovery`, `source_provider` = `okta`. **Okta facts do not write canonical graph records directly. Okta facts do not write app_user_identity_matches directly** — no direct write to apps/app_aliases/vendors/app_products/people/app_users/app_user_identity_matches.
- **ALLOWLIST construction (not blocklist stripping):** every fact is built from an explicit named allowlist of safe fields; the raw record is never spread; `settings` blob / `settings.signOn` (signing keys) / `_links` / `credentials` / `client_secret` / cookies / tokens are NEVER read — an unexpected or secret field cannot reach `fact_json`/`provenance_json` (tested). A domain/instance_domain is emitted ONLY from an explicit structured `settings.app.domain` field — never inferred from label/name/signOnMode/URL (negative test). Confidence is high (0.9) only because facts are anchored on deterministic Okta ids; `natural_key` derives from immutable Okta ids (stable to label/status changes). Email/login normalization REUSES `resolution.ts`'s `normalizeEmail` (trim + lowercase only — `Jane.Doe@Acme.com` == `jane.doe@acme.com`; `jane.doe` ≠ `janedoe`; `jane+test` ≠ `jane`).
- **Provider payload tenant_id is not trusted. Tenant scope comes from authenticated/server context** — every fact's tenant_id is the authenticated argument (a payload tenant_id is never read); `emitOktaDiscoveryFacts` stages nothing without an authenticated tenant; malformed records are skipped (fail closed). No `app_alias` fact type / `sso_app_id` alias / group-membership source plumbing is invented — documented as gaps for a later schema PR.
- **No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No API key is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read. No connector sync is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is exposed to users. No browser-accessible service-role request path is added. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run. Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§75), 41 (§86), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` **472/472**; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (18 routes); `check-auth-safety`/`check-migration-safety`/`check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `test-rls.sh` **492** (`ALL ORG-RLS ASSERTIONS PASSED`); `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff (1828 lines — no schema change); `node --check` on all four verifier scripts OK; linked ref confirmed `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai` (local read); no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays; no new dependency; scope: Okta emitter/tests + guard hint + docs.

### PR #150 — Add deterministic app user identity match write path · 2026-06-23
- **Category:** the first canonical USER-identity write — **migrations `0027` (app_user_identity_matches INSERT+UPDATE write policies, NO DELETE) + `0028` (the `UNIQUE(tenant_id, app_user_id)` integrity constraint — one app_user → at most one person per tenant) + server-only `identity-match-write.ts` + `identity-match-write.test.ts` (21 tests) + T49 (DB-level persisted-state idempotency) + the `no-client-import` guard + docs.** No dependency; **no app-graph/app_alias/vendor/product write, no service-role client, no provider API, no token/credential, no `connector_secrets`, no HTTP route, no hosted command, no production mutation.** Tests 425 → **446**; RLS **478 → 492**; types unchanged 1828 (policies only); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Deterministic app_user to person identity-match write path is added. This is the first canonical user matching write path** (doc 42 §72, doc 41 §83). `identity-match-write.ts` connects an app_user to a person on deterministic, tenant-safe evidence only, through the injected user-scoped (authenticated, RLS) `IdentityMatchWriteStore` — never service-role — so canonical user rollups can count DISTINCT people instead of raw app accounts. Migration `0027` adds the write surface (editors INSERT + editors UPDATE on `app_user_identity_matches`, **NO DELETE** — the `0004` directive); migration `0028` adds the `UNIQUE(tenant_id, app_user_id)` integrity constraint that makes the editor INSERT policy safe. Functions: `applyDeterministicIdentityMatches` / `repointIdentityMatch`.
- **Only deterministic identity evidence may write. Exact normalized email matches may write. Exact provider/external identity matches may write where tenant-bound. Display-name-only matches do not write. Domain-only matches do not write. Probabilistic matches do not auto-write. Ambiguous matches do not auto-write. Multiple candidate people route to review/no-write. Existing conflicting matches are not overwritten.** A false person-merge is more expensive than leaving an app_user unmatched; no email/external id, multiple people, tenant mismatch, existing-different-person, or malformed input all fail closed. The deterministic auto match is recorded with `match_method` (`auto_exact_email`/`auto_identity_account_email`/`auto_external_id`) distinguishing it from a future human-confirmed match.
- **Identity matching is tenant-scoped. Repeated deterministic identity match runs are idempotent. Repeated runs do not create duplicate app_user_identity_matches** (the `(tenant_id, app_user_id)` UNIQUE from `0028` — `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, app_user_id) DO NOTHING`; the `0001` `(app_user_id, person_id)` pair UNIQUE is kept, but **`(tenant_id, app_user_id)` is the one that backs the idempotency invariant and prevents false person double-matches** — the DB rejects a second match for the same app_user to a different person, not only the helper). **Unmatch/repoint is modeled and non-destructive. Unmatch/repoint does not delete app_users, people, identity_accounts, apps, contracts, invoices, or audit history** (`repointIdentityMatch` UPDATEs person_id; no DELETE policy). **No app graph write is implemented in this PR. No app_alias write is implemented in this PR.**
- **T49** (RLS **478 → 492**; types 1828 0-diff) proves the persisted-state guarantees on real Postgres: `app_user_identity_matches` policy set is EXACTLY {SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE} (no DELETE/ALL); re-inserting the same match → unchanged count; a SECOND match for the same (tenant, app_user) to a DIFFERENT person is REJECTED (`unique_violation`, the false-double-match guard — the original is preserved); repoint is a non-destructive UPDATE (app_user/person/app survive); Tenant B cannot read or insert a Tenant A match. Email normalization is trim + lowercase only (Jane.Doe@Acme.com == jane.doe@acme.com; jane.doe ≠ janedoe; jane+test ≠ jane). Helper tests prove deterministic email/identity-account/external-id matches, no-evidence/display-name/domain-only/multiple-people/tenant-mismatch/conflict fail-closed, idempotency, and the no-app-graph/no-service-role/no-secret/no-route surface.
- **No provider API call is made. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No API key is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read. No connector sync is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is exposed to users. No browser-accessible service-role request path is added. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run. Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. RISK-007 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§72), 41 (§83), 09, 00.

### PR #147 — Add deterministic resolver write path · 2026-06-23
- **Category:** the FIRST canonical-graph mutation — **migration `0026` (alias natural-key UNIQUE) + server-only `resolver-write.ts` + `resolver-write.test.ts` (15 tests) + T48 (DB-level persisted-state idempotency) + the `no-client-import` guard + docs.** No dependency; **no service-role client, no `app_user_identity_matches` write, no provider API, no token/credential, no `connector_secrets`, no HTTP route, no hosted command, no production mutation.** Tests 410 → **425**; RLS **458 → 478**; types unchanged 1828 (constraint only); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Deterministic resolver write path is added. This is the first canonical graph mutation path** (doc 42 §69, doc 41 §80). `resolver-write.ts` reads staged `discovery_facts`, runs the pure #140 resolver, and writes only deterministic outputs through the injected user-scoped (authenticated, RLS) `CanonicalGraphWriteStore` — never service-role. **Only deterministic resolver outputs may write. Probabilistic matches do not auto-write. Ambiguous matches do not auto-write. Low-confidence matches remain reviewable. False splits are safer than false merges.** A missing instance discriminator, a probabilistic/name-only signal, or a conflict (alias already resolves elsewhere, or the instance already has a different `canonical_app_id`) → review, never an overwrite. Functions: `applyDeterministicResolution` / `revertCanonicalAppAssignment` / `repointAppAlias`.
- **Resolver writes are idempotent. Repeated staged fact runs do not create duplicate app_alias rows. Repeated staged fact runs do not create duplicate vendor/product/app records. Runners must upsert on natural keys, not blindly insert.** Migration `0026` adds the alias natural key `UNIQUE(tenant_id, alias_type, alias_value)` (vendor/product keys from `0024`) → `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` idempotent. **Arrival order must not change persisted resolver state. Distinct app instances must not be collapsed into one app row. Jira Flywheel and Jira Perpetua remain separate app rows. Slack multi-source facts converge without duplicate aliases when deterministic evidence is sufficient. A weak signal followed by deterministic evidence must not create a parallel app. Unmerge/repoint is modeled for deterministic assignments. Unmerge/repoint does not delete historical users, contracts, or invoices.**
- **T48** (RLS **458 → 478**; types 1828 0-diff; migration-safety passes) proves persisted-state idempotency on real Postgres (the same deterministic write run twice → unchanged app_alias/app_products/vendors counts via the natural-key UNIQUE), the Flywheel ≠ Perpetua split (two aliases, one product, two apps rows), and non-destructive unmerge/repoint (clearing `canonical_app_id` / repointing an alias leaves apps + app_users/contracts/invoices intact), plus Tenant B cannot read Tenant A's resolver-written aliases. Helper tests prove deterministic-only writes, conflict→review, convergence, order-independence, weak-then-deterministic, tenant isolation, and the no-secret/no-route/no-matches surface.

- **Category:** RLS-backed ingestion staging boundary (the first safe write path for validated facts) — **migration `0025` (tenant-scoped `discovery_facts` table + RLS + indexes) + server-only helper `discovery-fact-staging.ts` + `discovery-fact-staging.test.ts` (15 tests) + T47 + generated types + the `no-client-import` guard + docs.** No dependency; **no live resolver, no canonical-graph/canonical_app_id/app_alias/app_user_identity_match write, no provider API, no token/credential, no `connector_secrets`, no service-role client, no `connector_runner` grant, no API route/UI, no hosted command, no production mutation.** Tests 369 → **384**; RLS **446 → 458**; types 1744 → 1828; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

Token-bearing facts are rejected before persistence. Nothing populates apps.canonical_app_id yet.** **The resolver is the moat engine that will assemble validated discovery signals into the canonical app graph.** `resolution.ts` adds pure types (`DiscoveryResolutionInput`/`AppResolutionCandidate`/`IdentityMatchCandidate`/`ResolutionDecision`/`ResolutionConfidence`) + in-memory helpers (`classifyResolutionConfidence`/`explainResolutionDecision`/`appResolutionSignals`/`sameOperationalInstance`/`normalizeEmail`/`identityMatchSignals`).
- **Resolver matching is deterministic-first and probabilistic-second. Low-confidence matches route to human review.** Deterministic keys (instance_domain/external_instance_id/instance_url/provider-app-id/oauth-client-id/sso-app-id/known-domain/explicit vendor-product ids) may auto-assign; fuzzy similarity never auto-merges; unknown/ambiguous fails closed to `human_review`. **Discovery re-runs must be idempotent. Runners must upsert on natural keys, not blindly insert. instance_domain and external_instance_id are future merge/no-merge keys. Same vendor/product does not mean same operational app instance. Distinct app instances must not be collapsed into one app row. Atlassian/Jira/Flywheel and Atlassian/Jira/Perpetua must remain distinct app instances.**
- **Identity matching:** **app_user_identity_matches links app_user_id to person_id. There is no identity_account_id on app_user_identity_matches. No identity_account_id is introduced.** Deterministic-first (exact normalized email / verified external id) then secondary hints → review. **Canonical user rollups must count distinct person_id after identity matching, not sum app_users naively. Per-instance counts may use app_users.**
- **No live resolver job is implemented. No app graph writes are implemented. No canonical_app_id write is implemented. No app_alias write is implemented. No app_user to person match write is implemented. No provider API call is made. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. ### PR #137 — Add canonical app instance graph design · 2026-06-22
- **Category:** net-new schema/design foundation (product moat) — **migration `0024` (3 tenant-scoped tables + apps instance/canonical fields + RLS + indexes) + T46 + generated types + docs.** No app code (only generated `database.types.ts`); no dependency; **no app-graph write, no resolver, no provider API, no token/credential, no `connector_secrets` grant/policy, no `identity_account_id`, no hosted command, no production mutation.** RLS **424 → 446**; types 1553 → 1744; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Canonical vendor/product/instance graph design is added. This is net-new moat work, not old-app parity restoration** (doc 42 §59, doc 41 §70). **The old app was also flat at the app-document level. The old app had manual overlap-analysis grouping, not automatic canonical app resolution** (old manual overlap groups may need inventory/port later if OMC depends on them). Migration `0024_canonical_app_instance_graph.sql` adds the schema/design foundation for `vendor → canonical app/product → app instance/site/workspace → users/contracts/invoices/metrics`.
- **apps remains the operational app instance/site/workspace row. Distinct app instances must not be collapsed into one app row. Canonical matching groups related apps for roll-up reporting without erasing instance boundaries.** Three tenant-scoped tables (same-tenant integrity via UNIQUE(id,tenant_id)+composite MATCH-SIMPLE FKs; RLS = members read + editors INSERT + editors UPDATE, NO DELETE — the 0004-hardened posture): `vendors`, `app_products` (canonical), `app_aliases` (provenance + audit/review fields reusing the app_user_identity_matches pattern: confidence numeric(5,2)/review_status/reviewed_by/reviewed_at/provenance). **Structured instance identity fields are added to apps:** nullable `canonical_app_id` (same-tenant FK to app_products) + `instance_domain` + `external_instance_id` + `instance_url`. **instance_domain and external_instance_id are future merge/no-merge discriminators.** Indexes for the new tables + apps canonical/instance fields.
- **Multi-instance (Atlassian):** Atlassian → Jira/Confluence/Bitbucket → Jira/Flywheel (flywheel.atlassian.net), Jira/Perpetua (perpetua.atlassian.net), Confluence/Flywheel — separate apps rows under one canonical product, never collapsed. **Existing app_contracts already supports one contract linked to many app instances. No replacement for app_contracts is added. One-invoice-split-across-orgs is documented as future work only.** **Canonical user rollups must count distinct person_id after identity matching, not sum app_users naively. Canonical rollups depend on the app_user to person matching engine.** Future resolver (documented, NOT implemented): deterministic keys first (instance_domain/external_instance_id/domain/provider-app-id/oauth-client-id/sso-app-id), owner/paying/responsible org influences merge, same product but different instance discriminator stays separate apps, low confidence → human review, unmerge by repointing aliases/canonical_app_id (not rewriting history). **No automatic resolver is implemented.**
- **T46** (RLS suite **424 → 446**; types 1553 → 1744; migration-safety passes): the 3 new tables RLS-enabled with EXACTLY {SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE} policies (no DELETE/ALL); functional tenant isolation (Tenant A member reads its vendor/product, Tenant B member cannot, cross-tenant insert RLS-denied); the apps canonical/instance columns exist; app_contracts unchanged (composite `(app_id, contract_id)` PK intact, no canonical/instance columns); **No identity_account_id is introduced**; app_aliases audit fields exist; connector_secrets still zero policies. **No app graph writes are implemented. No provider API call is made. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No connector credentials are stored. ### PR #134 — Add graph scale indexes for discovery matching · 2026-06-22
- **Category:** schema-grounded index foundation (graph scale) — **migration `0023` (36 CREATE INDEX, index-only) + T45 + docs; NO column/schema change, NO grant, NO policy, NO RLS-behavior change, NO app/UI/route change, NO dependency.** No app-graph write; no connector_secrets/token/provider activity; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation. RLS **413 → 424**; types 0-diff (1553 lines); no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Graph-scale discovery indexes are added. The indexes are schema-grounded against the current 0001 core graph tables** (doc 42 §56, doc 41 §67) — `0023_graph_scale_discovery_indexes.sql` adds 36 indexes preparing the core graph tables for high-volume Okta/Google/Microsoft discovery data before those connectors write real volume.
- **What they support:** **The indexes support tenant-scoped RLS hot paths** (`tenant_memberships_user_tenant_status_idx` + every graph table's tenant/status). **The indexes support high-volume app_users discovery data** (tenant/app, status, external_user_id). **The indexes support app-user and identity-account matching** on person_id — **the app_user_identity_matches model is app_user to person, not app_user to identity_account** (identity_accounts → person via person_id). **The indexes support case-insensitive email matching through lower(email) and lower(primary_email)** (app_users/identity_accounts/people). **The indexes support vendor/app-name normalization through lower(vendor_name) and lower(name)** (apps/contracts). Plus owning-org joins (apps procurement_owner_org_id/paying_org_id/responsible_org_id; contracts procurement_org_id/paying_org_id) + invoices/app_contracts/license rollups.
- **Schema-grounded:** graph tables have no `organization_id`; email + external-id columns differ per table (verified against `0001`). **No identity_account_id column is introduced** (there is none). `app_user_identity_matches` already has UNIQUE(app_user_id, person_id) → only tenant + person_id indexes added (no duplicate). **No canonical vendor/app registry is implemented in this PR** (next design/schema PR). **No app graph write is implemented.** Plain (non-CONCURRENT) CREATE INDEX is correct (tables near-empty, lands before volume); **if ever deferred until after discovery data loads, a future index migration MUST use CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY** (cannot run in a transaction block).
- **T45** (RLS suite **413 → 424**; types 0-diff 1553 lines; migration-safety passes): re-asserts a representative sample of the 36 indexes exists (RLS hot path, app_users/identity_accounts discovery, `*_person_idx` match indexes, the lower(email)/lower(name)/lower(primary_email) FUNCTIONAL indexes, lower(vendor_name) normalization, owning-org joins, invoices/app_contracts/license) + the schema-grounding guards (no `identity_account_id` on app_user_identity_matches; app_user → person; identity_accounts → person via person_id). **No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read. No provider API call is made. No connector sync is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is exposed to users. No browser-accessible service-role request path is added. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. ### PR #133 — Add app discovery connector architecture · 2026-06-22
- **Category:** connector taxonomy / app-graph architecture (gated vault) — **safe registry metadata/types + a pure normalization helper + tests + docs; NO migration, NO dependency, NO app/UI/route change, no DB write, wired to nothing live.** No credential storage; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; no provider API; no token exchange; no app-graph DB write; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation.** RLS unchanged **413**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **App discovery connector architecture is recorded. Discovery connectors are modeled separately from deep provider sync runners. App graph normalization is the bridge between discovery signals and ID Caddie app records** (doc 42 §55, doc 41 §66). Shifts the roadmap from one-provider Slack plumbing to the broader "connect many apps quickly" discovery architecture: **discovery connectors are intended to discover many SaaS apps quickly through identity and core systems; deep provider sync runners remain provider-specific and will be added one at a time. The old scraper model is being replaced with discovery connectors plus provider sync runners.** Safe metadata/types + a pure helper ONLY — no provider is functional.
- **Registry taxonomy (`provider-registry.ts`, extended):** `ConnectorProviderDefinition` gains `kind: ConnectorKind` (identity_provider_discovery / deep_provider_sync / spend_invoice_discovery / import_source / browser_extension_discovery / manual_source) + `discoveryCapabilities: DiscoveryCapability[]` (discover_apps / discover_assigned_users / discover_groups / discover_login_activity / discover_domains / discover_owners / discover_sso_metadata / discover_usage_signals / discover_spend_signals / import_app_inventory — display labels). **Okta is added as an inert future identity-provider discovery connector. Google Workspace is added as an inert future identity-provider discovery connector. Microsoft Entra is added as an inert future identity-provider discovery connector** (status `future`, disabled, NO code/OAuth-URL/token/API). Slack stays a `deep_provider_sync` skeleton (existing metadata intact). Helpers (fail closed on unknown): `listDiscoveryProviders`/`listDeepSyncProviders`/`getProviderDiscoveryCapabilities`/`isDiscoveryProvider`/`isDeepSyncProvider`. No provider is ready; discovery providers have no deep-sync read capabilities and cannot sync or exchange tokens.
- **App-graph normalization (`app-discovery.ts`, types + one pure helper):** `DiscoveredAppSignal`/`AppMatchStatus`/`NormalizedAppCandidate` + `normalizeDiscoveredAppSignals(signals)` — groups by a normalized key (domain else name), merges sources/external ids, takes the max assigned-user count, scores naive confidence + match status. Deterministic + in-memory: **writes NO DB / no app-graph row, calls NO provider, stores NO credential.**
- **+12 tests (313 → 325)**; RLS unchanged **413** (no migration); types 0-diff; no dependency. Server-only (sentinel + `no-client-import` guard; both modules pure — zero imports); not imported by any app route. Tests: the 3 inert identity-discovery entries; discovery vs deep-sync classified separately + disjoint; Slack stays a non-functional deep-sync skeleton; no provider ready; discovery providers expose safe metadata only; unknown fails closed; the normalizer merges/keeps-distinct/fail-soft + imports nothing + writes no DB; registry purity (no fetch/createClient/process.env/connector_secrets/service_role/token). **No Okta connector is functional. No Google Workspace connector is functional. No Microsoft Entra connector is functional. No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read. No provider API call is made. No connector sync is implemented. - **Category:** Slack connector gate — authorize-time replay-row persist (gated vault) — **a server-only persist helper over an injected DB-insert seam + tests + docs; NO migration, NO dependency, NO app/UI/route change, library-only.** No credential storage; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; no token exchange; no Slack API; deny-all posture untouched; no global service-role client; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation.** RLS unchanged **387**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

Slack authorize creates a replay-protection row for a future callback consume step** (doc 42 §50, doc 41 §61) — `src/lib/server/connector-vault/providers/slack-authorize-pending.ts` composes the §49 Slack authorize-URL builder with the `oauth_pending` replay shape. **Slack remains non-functional for real connections.** It exchanges NO code, stores NO token/credential, touches NO `connector_secrets`, calls NO Slack API, marks NO connector connected, creates NO sync run.
- **Injected INSERT seam (no migration, no service-role client):** `oauth_pending` is Tier-2 deny-all to anon/authenticated (`0020`) and `connector_runner` has SELECT+UPDATE but **deliberately NOT INSERT** (`0021` deferred authorize-time create), so a request-path client cannot write this row. The privileged INSERT is delegated to an injected `SlackPendingInserter` (the runner-identity-backed inserter + its future INSERT grant is a later PR); tests inject a mock — no live DB write, no credentials. ### PR #127 — Add Slack OAuth authorize callback skeleton · 2026-06-22
- **Category:** first provider-specific connector module (gated vault) — **a server-only Slack OAuth authorize/callback skeleton + tests + docs; NO migration, NO dependency, NO app/UI/route change, wired to nothing.** No credential storage; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; no token exchange; no Slack API call; no browser-accessible service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation.** RLS unchanged **387**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Slack OAuth authorize/callback skeleton is added. The Slack provider remains non-functional for real connections** (doc 42 §49, doc 41 §60) — `src/lib/server/connector-vault/providers/slack-oauth.ts`, the first provider-specific module. It builds the Slack authorize-redirect URL and classifies the Slack callback, integrating the existing `oauth-state` signer + `oauth_pending` replay shape + the registry (§48). It exchanges NO code, stores NO token/credential, touches NO `connector_secrets`, calls NO Slack API, marks NO connector connected.
- **Authorize builder** `buildSlackAuthorizeUrl(input)` → `{ ok, url, stateJti, nonceHash, expiresAt } | { ok:false, reason }`: builds `https://slack.com/oauth/v2/authorize?client_id&scope&redirect_uri&state` with a SIGNED state (`createOAuthState`). `client_id` is INJECTED (server-only config / explicit input — never hardcoded, never read from env here); `redirect_uri` validated (absolute HTTPS only); scopes default to the registry's Slack DISPLAY scopes. - **Callback classifier** `classifySlackCallback(searchParams, opts)` → safe outcome (`provider_error` [Slack `?error=` cancel — raw value never surfaced] / `not_configured` [no signer wired — skeleton default] / `invalid` [wrong_provider/missing_code/ or an oauth-state reason] / `received` [valid callback — returns ONLY the future-consume keys stateJti/nonceHash, both one-way hashes]). Validates the signed state via `validateOAuthState`; checks `code` PRESENCE only (value never read/returned/logged); **NO token exchange, NO Slack call, NO `connector_secrets` write, NO connector marked connected.**
- **+15 tests (273 → 288)**; RLS unchanged **387** (no migration); types 0-diff; no dependency. - **Category:** server-only provider abstraction (gated vault) — **a pure-metadata registry module + one inert Slack entry + tests + docs; NO migration, NO dependency, NO app/UI change, wired to nothing.** No credential storage; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; no token exchange; no provider API; no browser-accessible service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation.** RLS unchanged **387**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

Slack is added as the first inert provider skeleton** (doc 42 §48, doc 41 §59) — the first connector implementation step after the §47 no-real-token dry-run gate cleared. `src/lib/server/connector-vault/provider-registry.ts` ships PURE, SAFE METADATA only. **No provider connector is functional yet.**

### PR #116 — Add oauth pending consume path · 2026-06-21
- **Category:** server-only model + injected executor contract (gated vault) — **pure consume logic + injected runner executor + tests + docs; NO execution path, NO migration, NO dependency, wired to nothing.** No credential storage; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; oauth_pending deny-all preserved; no browser-accessible service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation.** RLS unchanged **352**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Server-only oauth_pending consume path is added. The consume path performs atomic single-use consumption** (doc 42 §38, doc 41 §49) — the §16/§32.3 single-use consume, the last replay-store prerequisite before a first connector can be sketched: `src/lib/server/connector-vault/oauth-pending-consume.ts`. Stores nothing; wired to nothing. **Oauth_pending remains not directly readable or writable by anon or authenticated users.**
- **Deny-all preserved, no migration, no service-role request path.** `oauth_pending` is Tier-2 deny-all (`0020`, T42), so a request-path (`authenticated`) client cannot touch it. The module ships the PURE consume LOGIC + result classification and delegates the privileged DB write to an **injected `OAuthPendingConsumer`** (the runner-identity-backed executor — a real DB impl, backed by a `SECURITY DEFINER` accessor / the runner's connection, never reachable from request/browser code, is a later gated PR). **No browser-accessible service-role path is added; no migration is needed; the RLS suite stays 352.**
- **Atomic single-use.** The executor's `runAtomicConsume` runs ONE statement (documented reference SQL: `update public.oauth_pending set consumed_at = $now where state_jti/nonce_hash/tenant_id/provider match, connector_id is not distinct from $connector_id [null-safe], consumed_at is null, expires_at > $now returning id, state_jti, consumed_at`). Success = exactly one row changed → `{ ok: true, consumed }`; a concurrent second callback consumes nothing. On 0 rows, `consumeOAuthPending` does a READ-ONLY classify (by the unique `state_jti`) → a safe reason code (not_found / already_consumed / expired / tenant_mismatch / provider_mismatch / connector_mismatch / nonce_mismatch / malformed_input), **never mutating again** so single-use is preserved. Identity mismatches reported before consumed/expired (forgery/confused-deputy signal).
- **Redaction.** A result carries only a safe reason CODE + non-secret metadata (`stateJti`, `consumedAt`) — never a raw nonce/state/code/secret (`nonce_hash` is the only nonce-derived input, a one-way hash, never echoed back). Pure (NO imports), server-only (sentinel + no-client-import guard), nothing logs.
- **+16 app tests (225 → 241)**; RLS suite unchanged **352** (no migration); types 0-diff. Tests: consume-exactly-one (+ one atomic mutation); second-consume `already_consumed`; fresh-connect (null) consume; every failure → its safe reason (incl. connector present-vs-fresh + nonce mismatch); malformed never reaches the mutation; missing-consumer throws typed; failure echoes no raw value; module purity; **the OAuth callback route still exchanges no code and stores no token**; T42/T39/T40 deny-all proofs unchanged. **No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No connector credentials are stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, deleted, or read. No connector sync is implemented. No provider connector is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is implemented. No manual or scheduled run action is implemented. No browser-accessible service-role path is added. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run. Connector vault is still not usable for real credentials until the remaining gated PRs are complete** (next: PR G — a first low-risk connector — only after the runner-identity-backed executor + the IAM/KMS grant + a real KEK alias are provisioned + staging-verified by a human). **Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§38), 41 (§49), 09, 00.

### PR #111 — Add oauth pending replay store · 2026-06-21
- **Category:** schema/RLS + pure server-only helper (gated vault) — **migration `0020` + deny-all RLS + T42 + helper + tests + types + docs; NO execution path.** No consume function/route/sync/provider/OAuth-exchange; **`connector_secrets` untouched; no service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation; no real secrets.** RLS **327 → 352**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

- **`public.oauth_pending`** — `tenant_id`/`organization_id?`/`connector_id?`/provider/`subject?`/`state_jti`/`nonce_hash` [sha256 — **raw nonce never stored**]/`intent`/`expires_at` NOT NULL/`consumed_at?`/`created_at`/`attempt_count`/`last_rejected_code?` (CHECK = the PR F safe reason set). **No raw nonce/state/code/token/secret/PKCE column.** Single-use `UNIQUE(state_jti)` + `UNIQUE(nonce_hash)`; same-tenant composite `(connector_id, tenant_id)` FK (MATCH SIMPLE — skipped when connector_id null) blocks cross-tenant binding.

The future server-only consume path (runner / `SECURITY DEFINER`, a later PR) does the atomic single-use UPDATE; `test-rls.sh` re-asserts the `oauth_pending` revoke after its blanket-grant crutch. **`service_role` is never used on a request path.**
- **Server-only helper** `src/lib/server/connector-vault/oauth-pending.ts` (PURE — only `node:crypto`): `hashOAuthValue` (deterministic sha256, so the future consume path hashes a returning nonce the same way) + `buildOAuthPendingRecord` (validates + returns the safe row, **hashing the raw nonce and never returning it**, rejecting secret-shaped fields). No DB/token-exchange/`connector_secrets`/Supabase/service-role; server-only (sentinel + no-client-import guard).
- **T42** (RLS suite **327 → 352**): deny-all (runtime + catalog exact-zero-privilege) + structural (RLS, zero policies, no secret column, `expires_at` NOT NULL, UNIQUE single-use rejects duplicates, composite-FK cross-tenant block) + `connector_secrets`/Tier-1 grants unchanged by `0020`. **+9 app tests** for the helper (184 → 193); `database.types.ts` regenerated (includes `oauth_pending`). **No OAuth code is exchanged for tokens. ### PR #110 — Record connector KMS replay-store decision · 2026-06-21
- **Category:** design/decision record — **docs-only.** No src/migration/script/test change; **no `connector_secrets`; no service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation; no real secrets.** RLS unchanged **327**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **KMS/key-provider decision is recorded. OAuth state signer decision is recorded. OAuth replay-store design is recorded** (doc 42 §32, doc 41 §43) — resolving the §17 open questions that gate any real credential storage. **No real connector credential storage is implemented. No OAuth token exchange is implemented. No access token is stored. No refresh token is stored. No connector secret material is inserted, updated, or deleted.** No migration (the `oauth_pending` schema is conceptual/design-only).
- **KMS/key-provider (§32.1):** production `ConnectorVaultKeyProvider` backed by an **external managed KMS** (default AWS KMS / GCP KMS) holding the KEK; **Supabase Vault/pgsodium rejected** for the KEK (co-locates key+ciphertext). KEK owned by the server-only runner identity (KMS `GenerateDataKey`/`Decrypt` only); `kekId` = non-sensitive KMS handle per wrapped DEK; rotation by alias (no mass re-encryption); unwrap failure **fails closed** (typed error, no key/plaintext); local-dev uses the in-memory test provider — **no committed keys, no prod secret read in tests**.
- **OAuth signer (§32.2):** server-only ≥32-byte HMAC secret from the host secret store (`CONNECTOR_OAUTH_STATE_SECRET`), never in repo/migration/client; rotation accepts {current, previous} keys for a grace window = max state TTL (≤10 min); `state` carries only the signature; failures return a safe reason code only.
- **`oauth_pending` replay store (§32.3, design-only):** Tier-2 table (`tenant_id`/provider/connector_id?/subject?/`state_jti`/`nonce_hash` [sha256, raw nonce never stored]/`expires_at`/`consumed_at?`/`created_at`/safe attempt+reason metadata); `UNIQUE(state_jti)`/`UNIQUE(nonce_hash)`; **single-use consume = one atomic UPDATE** (server-only); **RLS deny-all + zero anon/authenticated privilege** (mirrors `connector_secrets`); constant-time hash compare; scheduled expiry sweep; `connector.oauth.state.created/.consumed/.expired/.rejected` → append-only `audit_logs` (safe metadata only).
- **Gates (§32.4):** no real OAuth token storage before the replay store is implemented+tested; no real credential storage before the production KMS provider is implemented+tested; no provider connector before replay store + key provider + audit path complete; no browser credential form until the secret write path is explicitly reviewed; no production credential storage before staging verification.
- **No connector sync is implemented. No provider connector is implemented. No credential form is implemented. No connect/reconnect/disconnect action is implemented. No manual or scheduled run action is implemented. No service-role request path is added. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run. Connector vault is still not usable for real credentials until the remaining gated PRs are complete** (next: PR G first connector). **Connector implementation remains blocked. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§17/§32), 41 (§43), 09, 00.

- **Category:** server-only validation infra + inert route (gated vault PR F) — **state/nonce model + inert callback route + tests + docs; NO token exchange, NO credential storage, NO migration.** No provider call/sync; **`connector_secrets` never read/written; no service-role; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation; no real secrets.** RLS unchanged **327**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.

### PR #102 — Harden connector vault metadata grants · 2026-06-20
- **Category:** schema/RLS privilege hardening (gated vault, follow-up to PR A) — **migration + tests + docs; NO execution path.** No src change (grant-only → types 0-diff); **no service-role path; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation by the agent; no secrets.** RLS **292 → 318**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Staging verification of 0017 found broad metadata/run table grants that must be hardened before the connector sequence continues.** A human applied `0017` to staging (`ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai`): `connector_secrets` correct (RLS enabled, zero policies, no anon/authenticated privilege), but `connectors` + `connector_runs` held broad anon/authenticated `INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER/SELECT` — because `0017` did `grant select` but never `revoke`d the hosted-default grants (the `0015`/`0016` masking lesson; the harness re-assert had only revoked per-DML, masking it). **Connector secret material remained inaccessible to anon and authenticated users** (bug was Tier-1 only).
- **Connector metadata/run grants are being hardened to least privilege.** Migration `0018_harden_connector_vault_grants.sql` does `revoke all` from anon + authenticated on all three vault tables, then `grant select` to authenticated on `connectors` + `connector_runs` only. After it: authenticated = `connectors:SELECT`, `connector_runs:SELECT`, `connector_secrets:(none)`; anon = (none) everywhere — no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER for any request-path role. No write policy added; `service_role` untouched; idempotent. Proven by `org_rls_test.sql` **T40** (exact per-role privilege arrays + TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER negatives + tenant-scoped SELECT still works + cross-tenant SELECT still RLS-denied); the harness re-assert now mirrors `0018`.
- **Connector vault is still not usable. Connector implementation remains blocked. No connector credentials are stored. No connector sync is implemented. No encryption/decryption wrapper is implemented. No provider connector is implemented. No OAuth callback is implemented. No connector UI is implemented. No service-role request path is added. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run by the agent** (a human re-applies `0018` to staging then production; next gate is still PR C). **Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 42 (§25), 41 (§36), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` 117/117; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (16 routes); `check-auth-safety`/`check-migration-safety`/`check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `test-rls.sh` **318** (`ALL ORG-RLS ASSERTIONS PASSED`); `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff (grant-only); `node --check` on all three scripts OK; linked ref confirmed `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai` (local read); no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays. ### PR #99 — Design connector credential vault · 2026-06-20
- **Category:** security design — **docs-only.** No src/migration/script/package change; **no hosted command run; no production/staging mutation; no secrets; no service-role added.** RLS unchanged + stays **248**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked.
- **Connector credential vault design is drafted but not implemented. Connector implementation remains blocked until the vault design is reviewed and accepted.** New design doc [42_CONNECTOR_CREDENTIAL_VAULT_DESIGN](./42_CONNECTOR_CREDENTIAL_VAULT_DESIGN.md) (18 required sections) — the §32.3 Option-A prerequisite for any connector work and for RISK-007. Five load-bearing decisions: two-tier split (RLS-readable metadata vs a secret store with no `authenticated` grant + RLS deny-all), envelope encryption (per-secret DEK under AEAD, KEK in managed KMS, AAD-bound), server-only secret use (decrypt in a runner covered by `check-auth-safety`; client gets a safe-metadata DTO only), re-authorized execution (server-side tenant+role check every action; no blanket service-role), and auditable/reversible/killable (append-only audit, versioned+revocable secrets, kill-switch). Full threat model for a malicious Tenant-A user (read Tenant-B creds / cross-tenant sync / token exfil / browser leak / service-role exploit / refresh replay / callback abuse / role elevation / stale creds / audit poisoning) mapped to controls.
- **Schema + RLS are conceptual only — no migration, table, policy, or encryption code.** Carries the `0016`/T37 privilege-surface lesson (REVOKE broad + GRANT narrow + assert `has_table_privilege`). Sequencing gated on design acceptance; rollout staging-only, feature-flagged, human-verified.
- **No connector credentials are stored by this PR. No connector sync is implemented by this PR. No production data was touched. No hosted commands were run. No migrations were added. No RLS policies were changed. No service-role access was added. Connectors remain not built. AI / Analysis remains not built. Imports remain not built. Exports remain not built. Billing remains not built. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Hosted Auth/tenant-context is verified, but old-app replacement is not yet verified. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 41 (§33), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` 117/117; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (16 routes); `check-auth-safety`/`check-migration-safety`/`check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `test-rls.sh` **248**; `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff; `node --check` on all three scripts OK; linked ref confirmed `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai` (local read); no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays. ### PR #78 — Harden contract-file upload finalization · 2026-06-19
- **Category:** feature hardening (roadmap epic E09a) — **adds the narrow migration `0016` the existing schema required.** No script/package change; **no service-role on any request/browser path; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation; no secrets; no public bucket; no broad DELETE/UPDATE/`FOR ALL`.** No doc 17 §5 box ticked; **RISK-001 stays OPEN; cutover BLOCKED.**
- **Why:** PR #76 inserted the `files` row first, then uploaded the object, but `0013` had **no UPDATE policy** and `0015` granted only SELECT/INSERT — so successful uploads stayed `upload_status='pending'` and a failed object upload left an orphan `pending` row. Staging verification (recorded [41 §12](./41_FULL_PARITY_IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP.md)) found 3 `files` rows for contract `cccca111-…a1`, 2 with Storage objects + **1 orphan** (`68006ec4-…`, `has_storage_object=false`), all `pending`. **The existing schema truly could not finalize without an UPDATE capability**, so a narrow migration is justified.
- **Contract-file upload finalization is hardened. Successful uploads no longer remain ambiguous pending rows. Failed uploads are explicitly dispositioned or documented as blocked by current policy.**
  - **Migration `0016`** — a NARROW UPDATE policy `"uploader finalizes own file"` (USING + WITH CHECK = `uploaded_by = auth.uid() AND can_write_contract(contract_id, tenant_id)`; no `uploaded_by`/`tenant_id`/`contract_id` reassignment) + a **privilege correction**: `revoke update, delete, truncate on public.files from authenticated` then `grant update (upload_status)` — after it `authenticated` holds EXACTLY `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE(upload_status)` on `public.files` (no DELETE, no TRUNCATE). No DELETE/`FOR ALL` policy, no Storage-policy/bucket change, no service-role; SELECT/INSERT (`0015`) + `service_role` untouched. **(Correction during PR #78 review — see below.)**
  - **DAL** — on success: finalize `upload_status='uploaded'` (bucket/content_type/byte_size/sha256 were already persisted at insert); on object-upload failure: disposition the row `upload_status='failed'` (not an ambiguous `pending`); download now **only signs `upload_status='uploaded'` rows** (a `pending`/`failed` row → `not_ready`, never signed).
  - **UI** — clear status labels (Uploaded / Pending — not yet openable / Upload failed — not openable); **Open appears only for finalized rows**; still no `storage_path`/signed-URL text.
- **Privilege-grant CORRECTION (caught by staging verification before merge — doc 41 §13).** The first cut of `0016` used `grant update (upload_status)` alone, which is **additive**: staging verification (`has_table_privilege` / `information_schema.column_privileges`) found `authenticated` still holding **broad DELETE, TRUNCATE, and table-level UPDATE on every column** of `public.files` (no migration granted these — hosted setup did; TRUNCATE especially bypasses row-level logic). The corrected `0016` **revokes** `update, delete, truncate` then re-grants only `update (upload_status)`. The local `test-rls.sh` harness was also masking this (its blanket `grant ... on all tables` re-broadened `files` after the migrations); it now re-asserts the migration-intended `files` grants so the suite reflects the real hosted surface.
- **Security/tests:** **T36** proves the uploader-finalize ROW scoping (uploader-only; cross-tenant / cross-user updates and `uploaded_by`/`tenant_id` reassignment denied — now at the privilege layer too); new **T37** proves the `files` PRIVILEGE surface for `authenticated` (`has_table_privilege` no DELETE / no TRUNCATE; `has_column_privilege` UPDATE only on `upload_status`, denied on `tenant_id`/`contract_id`/`uploaded_by`/`storage_path`/`storage_bucket`/`original_filename`/`byte_size`/`content_type`/`sha256`); **T34c** now asserts DELETE is denied at the privilege layer; the stale T33d count + T34d "no UPDATE" assertion reconciled. RLS suite **222 → 248**. +1 app-layer test (77 total): finalize sets `uploaded`; failed disposition sets `failed`; download refuses non-finalized rows; DTO still never leaks `storage_path`. Two adversarial security reviews of `0016` were run.
- **Storage authorization remains necessary but not sufficient for cutover. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Invoices remain not built. PDF/AI extraction remains not built. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 03 (migration `0016`), `rls_test_plan.md` (T36), 41 (§12 + E09a), 09, 00.
- **Validation (local, verified):** `npm test` **77/77**; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (10 routes); `check-auth-safety`/`check-migration-safety`/`check-docs-updated`/`pr-review-summary` pass; `test-rls.sh` **248** (T37 now PROVES no DELETE/TRUNCATE + UPDATE only on `upload_status`, no longer masked); `gen-types-local.sh` 0-diff (policy/grant only); `node --check` on all three scripts OK; no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays.

### PR #76 — Add contract file attachment UI · 2026-06-19
- **Category:** feature (roadmap epic E09a) — **first implementation PR on the verified Storage boundary.** **No migration / no schema change** (reused `files` `0012`–`0015` + the private `contract-files` bucket + the existing RLS/Storage policies + `pdf-validation.ts`); RLS unchanged + stays **222**; `check-auth-safety` green; types 0-diff. **No service-role on any request/browser path; no hosted command; no production/staging mutation; no secrets; no public bucket; no broad/weakened policy.** No doc 17 §5 box ticked; **RISK-001 stays OPEN; cutover BLOCKED.**
- **Contract-file upload/download UI is partially implemented.** The contract detail page (`/contracts/[id]`) now has a **Files / Attachments** section: list existing files, upload a PDF, and open an attached file. Server-side only for every authorization decision:
  - **DAL `src/lib/data/contract-files.ts`** (user-scoped server client, never service-role): `list` (RLS tenant-member SELECT; DTO never exposes `storage_path`/signed URLs), `upload` (validate from **server-measured bytes** via `validateContractPdf` → resolve `tenant_id` **server-side** → **files-row-first** RLS INSERT (`can_write_contract`, `uploaded_by=auth.uid()`, server-derived path `contracts/{tenant_id}/{file_id}.pdf`, `sha256`) → then upload bytes to the private bucket (Storage policy `can_write_contract_file`)), and `getDownloadUrl` (RLS read first, then a **60-second** signed URL — generated only after authorization).
  - **Server actions** `file-actions.ts` (read bytes server-side from `FormData`); **client** `contract-files.tsx` (file-input state + loading/success/validation/failure states; opens the signed URL via `window.open` without rendering it).
- **Security:** cross-tenant denial is enforced by the existing RLS + Storage policies + the same-tenant composite FK (already proven by `org_rls_test.sql` **T34** + the hosted Storage REST verifier 14/14). Added app-layer tests (`contract-files.test.ts`, +9): non-PDF rejected before any DB call; server-derived tenant/path; files-row-first ordering (RLS-denied insert ⇒ no object upload); upload-failure handling; download not_found never signs; signed URL is 60 s; list DTO never leaks `storage_path`. No UPDATE/DELETE policy on the request path ⇒ a failed object upload leaves a `pending` row for a future worker (documented).
- **Storage authorization remains necessary but not sufficient for cutover. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Old-app parity is not complete. UI/UX parity is not complete. AI/API connector parity is not complete. RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED.** Updated docs 41 (E09 progress), 09, 00.

- **Category:** ops tooling — **script + docs only.** No src/migration/package change; **no hosted command run by this PR; no production/staging mutation; no secrets.** RLS unchanged + stays **222**; no doc 17 §5 box ticked; cutover stays **BLOCKED**; **RISK-001 stays OPEN**.
- **Adds `scripts/verify-staging-rls-suite.mjs`** — a staging-ref-guarded gate for the remaining doc 17 §5 boxes 5/8 sub-task (full `org_rls_test.sql` re-run against hosted). **Analysis:** the local suite's fixture setup is destructive (`truncate table` 17 core tables **incl. `public.audit_logs` restart identity cascade**, `delete from auth.users`, ~116 INSERT/~77 UPDATE/~70 DELETE, `set role`); `test-rls.sh` relies on a **disposable container**, not rollback. **Hosted staging RLS execution is prepared but not yet run. Raw `org_rls_test.sql` must not be run directly against hosted staging unless wrapped in a proven rollback-only, staging-ref-guarded runner** — and even rollback-only against the shared staging project is unsafe (`TRUNCATE` of `audit_logs` fires a statement-level event the row-level `reject_audit_mutation()` does not cover → would wipe append-only audit history; `delete from auth.users` touches the managed auth schema; ACCESS EXCLUSIVE locks on 17 live tables).
- **The runner:** hard-refuses unless linked ref is staging `ycdpzduxugdsffjqyoai`; hard-refuses if linked ref is production `dzbfxulvxchdemcettrx`; **detects the destructive statements and refuses the raw run against the shared staging project**. The script **connects to nothing** — even the explicit `RLS_RUN_TARGET=disposable-isolated` opt-in only **emits a rollback-only runbook** (snapshot key-table counts → `begin … rollback` → prove post==pre counts incl. `audit_logs` → dispose) for a human to run against a **separate disposable** project (never the shared staging project, never production). It handles no connection string and **prints no secrets/URLs** (a deliberate choice after adversarial review flagged an argv/stack secret-leak and a URL-spelling-not-identity guard on an in-script execution path). Safe alternate: a dedicated disposable Supabase project / branch DB. **Production must not be touched.**
- **No production project was touched. No secrets recorded.** This PR prepares the runner; it does not run it against hosted. **RISK-001 remains OPEN. Cutover remains BLOCKED. Upload is not automatically production-ready. Storage completion is necessary but not sufficient for cutover.** Updated docs 30 (§6 analysis + runner), 31 (cross-ref), 04 (RISK-001 context), 09 (handoff), 00 (status).

### PR #51 — Add contract file Storage authorization helpers · 2026-06-17
- **Category:** migration — adds **`0014_contract_file_storage_auth_helpers.sql` only**. **No `storage.objects` policy applied, no hosted Supabase command, no bucket/policy creation, no production touch, no upload route/action/UI, no signed-URL/AI/OCR, no app/package change.** Migrations now `0001`–`0014`; `0001`–`0013` stay staged, **`0014` is `verified-local` + `ci-enforced` but NOT yet staged**; cutover stays **BLOCKED**; **RISK-001 stays OPEN**; Storage authorization is **NOT verified**.
- **Adds the two public-schema predicates docs/22 §5's staging `storage.objects` policies will call** (the helpers, not the policies):
  - `public.can_write_contract_file(target_file_id uuid, target_tenant_id uuid)` → a `files` row exists for `(file_id, tenant_id)` **AND** `public.can_write_contract(f.contract_id, f.tenant_id)` is true (tenant owner/admin/editor OR procurement-org manager; **never `paying_org` beyond what `can_write_contract` already allows**).
  - `public.can_read_contract_file(target_file_id uuid, target_tenant_id uuid)` → a `files` row exists **AND** `public.is_tenant_member(f.tenant_id)` is true.
  - Both **`SECURITY DEFINER`, `stable`, `search_path = public`** — definer bypasses `files`-SELECT RLS so an org-only manager (write-not-read, the `0013` asymmetry) still authorizes; `auth.uid()` is the caller; no recursion (`storage.objects` is never referenced by `files` policies). Both **fail closed** on a missing/wrong `(file_id, tenant_id)`.
- **RLS tests (T35, suite `205 → 222`)** prove: tenant owner/admin/editor pass write; procurement-org manager passes write when contract-write authority allows; **paying-org-only manager, tenant viewer, cross-org manager, and cross-tenant user are DENIED write**; tenant member (owner + viewer) passes read; org-only manager / cross-tenant / non-member are DENIED read; nonexistent file id and wrong tenant id **fail closed** for both helpers.
- `database.types.ts` adds only the two functions (1283 → 1291). **`storage.objects` policies remain NOT applied** (`pg_policies … contract% = 0`); the next sanctioned hosted mutation is the reviewed Storage object-policy apply (staging only, explicit human approval, doc 22 §5) **after `0014` is merged + applied to staging** — an agent does not run it. Production `dzbfxulvxchdemcettrx` untouched.
- **Updated** docs 00/01/02/03/04/09/14 (current-state counts → 222/T35, migration list → `0014`), docs 22/25 (helper migration now exists; object policies still pending), doc 04 RISK-001 (helpers landed, policies not applied — stays OPEN).
- **Tests run (local, verified):** `npm test` 67/67; lint clean; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `next build` clean (routes unchanged, 10); `test-rls.sh` → **222** (`0001`–`0014`); `check-migration-safety`/`check-auth-safety`/`check-docs-updated` pass; `gen-types-local.sh` → only the two new functions; no `* 2.*`/`* 3.*` strays.

- **Category:** feasibility spike — **docs-only.** No migration, no Storage bucket, no Storage object policy, no `storage` shim, no upload action/UI/route, no signed-URL code, no AI/OCR, no service-role, no app code, no package, no generated-type change. - **Outcome: B — real local Storage object-RLS testing is NOT feasible in this repo's pipeline right now.** **Empirically proven** against a fresh `postgres:16-alpine` (the exact harness `test-rls.sh`/`gen-types-local.sh` use, `auth` shim + `0001`–`0013`): `storage.objects` does not exist, `storage.foldername()` errors `schema "storage" does not exist`, and `create policy ... on storage.objects` errors the same. So a `0014` storage-policy migration would **break both plain-Postgres harnesses**; and even a hand-installed `storage` schema would only test the SQL predicate, **not** storage-api enforcement (public/private, owner, signed URLs) — i.e. faking safety.
- **No fake shim, no untestable policies.** The faithful local path is the real storage-api via `supabase start` (a ~13-service stack), which is a separate/heavier CI infra decision (not bolted onto the fast `test-rls.sh`); the repo is not even `supabase init`'d.
